```
def _resample(seq, target):
   seq = np.asarray(seq,float)
   k=seq.size
   if target==k: return normalize(seq)
   if k==0: return normalize(ones)
   # if target larger: linear interp; if smaller: average or interp down
   x = np.linspace(0,1,k); y=np.linspace(0,1,target)
   out=np.interp(y,x,seq)
   # for downsample, interp smooth; okay
   return normalize(out)
```

```
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  logw = np.zeros(n)
```

```
def _gradient_polish(p, n, deadline, max_iters=2000):
   y=log(p)-mean
   base_step = 0.1
   for it: 
      val, gy, grad_info = obj_exact? Use small tau smooth or exact top weights.
      d = -gy
      norm = norm(d)
      if norm tiny break
      d = d/norm
      step = base_step
      while step > 1e-6 and iters:
         y_try = y + step*d   # descent since d=-g
         p_try=softmax(y_try)
         sc=_score_internal(p_try)
         if sc<sc_best* (1 - eps): accept; update base_step=max(base*1.05,0.5)
         step*=0.5
      if no accept: base*=0.7; if base<1e-4 break
```

Anticonv q_i=∑_k dV/db_k p_{k-i}. Then grad_p = 2 q. Then y_grad = p*(grad_p - dot). We can optimize with L-BFGS for a few iterations. This may flatten all lags, perhaps lower max. But might increase max at some Lagrange? Since average fixed; variance minimization could make distribution more uniform (score near2) if no skew? But starting parent near optimal, variance stage might move to symmetric lower variance but higher max? Need line search for variance only, not final score. Could harm. Use as optional start: run L2 optimization from parent, then exact max optimization; keep best by exact. If L2 leads to worse max, we still have best. But it might require time. We can do a short L2 stage from parent to maybe find different direction.

Actually minimizing variance may push to uniform (variance 0? Uniform b triangular not uniform, variance high? The true max-minimizer likely low variance too. Could help. Let's include one short L2 L-BFGS stage with e.g. 100 iterations from best, then max schedule. If exact score improves, keep; else revert. Since internal nonmonotonic allowed.

Implement L2 objective similar. It also uses FFT weights? dV/db length M. To compute q, use anti_conv with w=dV/db (which can be negative). w may have negative values; anti_conv with negative weights works (FFT linear). Good. No positivity required for gradient. But `dV/db` sum? b sum=1, c=mean => sum dV/db=0. Good.

Scale f = 2n * variance? For L-BFGS, objective values tiny? Var ~? b ~1/n, b² ~1/n², sum ~1/n. 2n*var ~1. Good. Use f=2n*var.

Gradient scale: f=2n Var. grad_p = 2n*2q? Actually variable p. y_grad = 2n*p*(gp - dot). gp=2q. Good.

Implement `_smooth_obj_y(x, n, L, M, two_n, kind, tau)` where kind 'max' or 'var'. If kind max, weights positive. If var, dV/db=2(b-c). Since c mean. f = two_n*var (where var=sum((b-c)^2)); gp=2*anti_conv(dvdb,p)*? If f=two_n*var, df/db=two_n*2(b-c); gp = anti_conv? We can set `db = two_n*2*(b-c)`, q=anti_conv(db,p), gp=2q. Good. But factor large; okay.

For max smooth: f=two_n*(cmax + tau log(S/M)). Let weights w. df/db=two_n*w. q=anti_conv(df/db,p) = two_n*anti_conv(w,p); gp=2q =4*two_n*anti_conv(w,p). Previously we set gp=2*qw and ygrad=two_n*p*(gp - dot) = two_n*p*(2*anti_conv(w?)?). Let's unify:
- If weights w sum1, f_max = two_n * (c + tau log(S/M)). df/db = two_n * w. grad_p_i = 2∑ df/db p = two_n*2*anti_conv(w,p). Call gp = two_n*2*anti_conv(w,p). Then y_grad = p*(gp - dot(p,gp)). This matches previous: previous gp_base=2*anti_conv(w,p), y_grad=two_n*p*(gp_base - dot(p,gp_base)). Dot(p,gp_base) =2*∑w b?; y_grad two_n*p*(2q - p·2q) = two_n*p*(...); yes. If we use gp=two_n*2q, dot= p·gp, y_grad=p*(gp-dot). Same. Good.

Helper `_normalize(arr)`:

```
return arr
```

For softmax p, always positive.

`_floor(p, eps)` for LP: `p = np.maximum(p, eps); p/=p.sum()`.

`_soft_y(p)`: `y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); y-=y.mean(); return y`

`_softmax(y)`: `y = y - y.max(); e=np.exp(y); s=e.sum(); if not finite or s==0 return uniform; p=e/s; p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum();` Maybe subtract mean before to improve? `y = y - y.mean()`? Since softmax invariant, okay; but if y large negative mean? Use max. We'll do:
```
y = np.asarray(y,float)
if not all finite: return uniform
y = y - np.max(y)
e=np.exp(y)
s=e.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: uniform
p=e/s
# floor tiny
p=np.where(p>1e-30, p, 1e-30); p/=p.sum()
return p
```

`_fft_len(m)`: `1 << (m-1).bit_length()`.

`_ctx(n)`: returns tuple (n,M,L,M, two_n). `L = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()`? Need M=2n-1, convolution length for p*w_rev = n+M-1=3n-2. So `L = np.fft.next_fast_len(3*n-2)` if available; else power of two. Use `1 << ( (3*n-2) -1).bit_length()`. For n=1? 3n-2=1, L=1. M=1. Slice T[n-1:2n-1] => T[0:1]. okay.
Use `np.fft.next_fast_len(3*n-2)` maybe faster (fftw? numpy has next_fast_len? `np.fft.next_fast_len` yes). Use for L. It returns int maybe not power2, convenient. For p FFT for b, need L≥2n-1; same L bigger. Use one L. Good. `L = np.fft.next_fast_len(3*n-2)`.

`_anti_conv(w, p, L)`:
```
M=len(w); n=len(p)
w_rev=np.zeros(L)
w_rev[:M]=w[::-1]
W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev)
P=np.fft.rfft(p)
T=np.fft.irfft(P*W, n=L)
return T[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
We can optimize by computing P once in objective and passing to anti. We'll write objective specific.

Objective functions with closure to avoid passing many:
```
def _make_objective(ctx, kind, tau):
  n,M,L,two_n=ctx
  def obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    p = _softmax(x)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)[:M]
    # if small negative clip
    if (b < 0).any(): b = np.clip(b,0,None)
    if kind == 'max':
       maxb=float(b.max())
       if not finite or maxb<=0: return 1e100,zeros
       diff=(b-maxb)/tau
       w=np.exp(diff)
       S=w.sum()
       if not finite or S<=0: w=np.ones(M)/M; S=M
       f=maxb + tau*math.log(S/M)
       w /= S
       # gp = two_n*2*anti(w,p)
       # compute anti using P already (for p)
       w_rev=np.zeros(L); w_rev[:M]=w[::-1]
       W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
       T=np.fft.irfft(P*W, n=L)
       q=T[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       gp = 4.0 * two_n * q  # since two_n*2q
       dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
       grad = p * (gp - dot)
       if not all finite: grad=zeros
       return two_n*f? Wait f already maybe not scaled. We defined f=maxb+...; objective value should be two_n*f (score_smooth). So val=two_n*f.
       return val, grad
    elif kind == 'var':
       c=b.mean(); d=b-c; var=float(np.dot(d,d))
       val=two_n*var
       db=two_n*2*d
       # q=anti(db,p)
       w_rev... db could negative; okay
       W=rfft(w_rev); T=irfft(P*W); q=...
       gp=2.0*anti(db,p) = 2*q? Instead we can call anti with db; gp=2*q.
       dot=dot(p,gp); grad=p*(gp-dot)
       return val, grad
  return obj
```
Need be careful with `two_n`: for var, `db=two_n*2*d`, `q=anti(db,p)`, `gp=2*q`. That means gp=two_n*4*anti(d,p). Good.
For max, `q=anti(w,p)`, gp=two_n*2*q. Good.

Could avoid computing `w_rev` dense with negative db? okay.

The objective value for max scaled by two_n: `val = two_n * (maxb + tau*math.log(S/M))`. tau is in b units (maxb). Good.

Gradient finite: `grad = p*(gp - dot)`. Dot with gp already scaled; result scaled. If p floor, sum grad maybe tiny nonzero due numerical; can subtract mean? For softmax gradient sum zero; due float may ~1e-15. We can `grad -= grad.mean()` to enforce. But if we subtract mean, changes function? Invariant, okay. Do `grad = grad - np.mean(grad)`.

Line search in L-BFGS with gradient sum zero; mean subtraction fine.

Potential overflow in exp(diff) if tau small and b-maxb due negative large -> underflow to0; max term 1. But if b contains maxb multiple, all good. If tau very small and maxb repeated? no issue. If tau underflows? min tau maybe 1e-6*maxb ~1e-9; diff for far lags -1000? underflow. okay.

Now exact internal score function:
```
def _internal_score(p, ctx):
 n,M,L,two_n=ctx
 P=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
 b=np.fft.irfft(P*P,n=L)[:M]
 b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
 return two_n*float(b.max())
 ```
If b all zero (should not) return 0.

For compare in optimization, use this. But FFT rounding may make score slightly different from evaluator; final use evaluator.

Now LP response function with dense A. For speed, maybe skip if n>800. Use if `linprog` available.
```
def _lp_response(p, ctx, upper=1e6, eps=1e-12):
 n,M,L,two_n=ctx
 p = np.maximum(p, eps); p/=p.sum()
 C = _internal_score(p,ctx)/two_n
 if C<=0: return None
 # Build A = p/C transposed? For row k: A[k,j]=p[k-j]/C
 scale=1.0/C
 A=np.empty((M,n),float)
 for k in range(M):
    if k<n:
       row = p[:k+1][::-1]
    else:
       row = p[k-n+1:n][::-1]
    A[k]=row*scale
 # c
 c = -np.ones(n) - 1e-5*p
 b_ub=np.ones(M)
 bounds=(0.0, upper)
 try: res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
 except...
 if res success:
    g=res.x; g=np.where(finite&>0,g,0); s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s
 return None
```
Potential memory A for n=512 1023*512*8~4.2MB, fine. For n=1024 2k*1k*8=16MB, okay maybe but slower. We can set `if n>700: return None` to save. If n=512, okay. If parent length maybe other, limit 800.

```
 return bestp,bestv
```

Now random starts and jitters. Use `rng`. Smooth noise via FFT:
```
def _smooth_noise(n, rng):
  noise = rng.normal(size=n)
  # low pass filter with Hamming window kernel
  k=5
  kernel=np.ones(k)/k
  noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
  noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
  noise-=noise.mean()
  return noise
```
Maybe use small sigma. For random full start:
```
def _random_semi(n,rng):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  logw=np.zeros(n)
  # random smooth
  logw += 0.7*smooth_noise(n,rng)
  # random low order polynomial
  logw += rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3) + rng.uniform(-1,1)*(x-0.5) + rng.uniform(-1,1)*(x-0.5)**2
  # random Fourier low
  for m in range(1,4):
     amp=rng.uniform(0,0.5)
     phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
     logw += amp*np.cos(2π m x + phase)
  p=softmax(logw)
```
This uses noise smooth over n. For n=512, sigma maybe 0.3. Good.

But random noise over full length may be too high-frequency? Convolve twice helps. Could use Gaussian filter: `kernel = np.exp(-((np.arange(21)-10)**2)/(2*3**2)); kernel/=kernel.sum(); for several passes`. We'll implement simple box passes.

Jitter start from base:
```
def _jitter_start(base, rng, sigma):
  noise = _smooth_noise(len(base), rng)
  y = np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-30)) - np.mean(...)
  y += sigma*noise
  return _softmax(y)
```

```
def _make_starts(n, rng, parent_arr, do_random):
  starts=[]
  if parent_arr is not None:
    starts.append(_resample(parent_arr, n))
  # add jitter
  if len(starts)>=1:
    for sigma in (0.15, 0.4 if n<=768 else 0.1):
       if do_random: starts.append(_jitter_start(starts[0], rng, sigma))
       if len(starts)>=max_starts: break
  if do_random:
    starts.append(_random_start(n, rng))
  return starts[:max_starts]
```

Optimization routine signature:
```
def _optimize_p(p0, n, rng, deadline, lp_iters=0, base_iter=250):
   p0=_normalize(p0)
   p_best=p0; val_best=_internal_score(p0,ctx)
   consider(p_best)
   y_best=log_p(p_best)
   # LP
   if lp_iters and linprog and n<=800:
      for it in range(lp_iters):
         if now > deadline-30: break
         g=_lp_response(p_best,ctx)
         ... update p_best, val_best; y_best=log_p(p_best)
   # L2 coarse maybe if now>deadline-120
   if minimize and n<=2048:
      # L2 from best? Use work = p_best
      y_work=y_best
      # var stage only if time
      if now < deadline-150:
         y_work = _run_minimize(y_work, 'var', 0, iters=base_iter//2, deadline=deadline-30)
         p_work=_softmax(y_work)
         val_work=_internal_score(p_work)
         if val_work < val_best: update best
         # maybe keep work if val_work < val_best*1.02 else p_best
         y_work = log_p(p_best if val_work > val_best else p_work) ?
   # max schedule
   rels = [0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.006,0.0025,0.001]
   no_improve=0
   y_work = y_best
   # We may need separate work p if not best. Track p_work.
   p_work=p_best
   for rel in rels:
      if now > deadline-40: break
      # if p_work score much worse than best and not leading to improvement, reset to best
      val_work=_internal_score(p_work)
      if val_work > val_best*1.05 + 1e-12:
         p_work=p_best; y_work=y_best
      c = val_work / (2*n)
      if c <=0: break
      tau = c*rel
      iters = base_iter
      # reduce if remaining
      if now > deadline-120: iters = max(40, iters//2)
      y_work = _run_minimize(y_work, 'max', tau, iters, deadline-40)
      p_work=_softmax(y_work)
      val_work=_internal_score(p_work)
      consider if val_work<best (but consider global evaluates authority; call maybe every stage for promise)
      if val_work < val_best*(1-1e-10):
         p_best=p_work; val_best=val_work; y_best=y_work; no_improve=0
         consider(p_best)
      else:
         no_improve += 1
         # maybe try from best if no improve for coarse?
         if no_improve >= 3 and rel < 0.05:
             y_work=y_best
             # maybe break
   # final active polish if time
   if now < deadline-90 and n<=1024:
       p_pol = _polish_gradient(p_best, deadline-80, iters=2000)
       if score<best consider
   return p_best, val_best
```

Detailed helper functions:

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(raw):
    a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a /= s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a
```
If all zeros except? okay.

`_make_ctx(n)`:
```
def _make_ctx(n):
    n=int(n)
    M=2*n-1
    L=int(np.fft.next_fast_len(3*n-2))
    if L < M: L=int(np.fft.next_fast_len(M))
    return (n,M,L,2.0*n)
```
For n=1, 3n-2=1, L=1; M=1.

`_softmax(y)`:
```
def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
       return np.ones(y.size)/y.size
    y = y - np.max(y)
    # maybe remove mean? not needed
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: uniform
    p = e/s
    # floor and renorm
    p = np.where(p > 1e-300, p, 1e-300)
    s = p.sum(); p /= s
    return p
```
Floor 1e-300 may underflow in FFT? exp in FFT of p values min 1e-300, fine but no effect. Renorm sum slight >1. Good. For n large, 1e-300*n/n okay.

`_log_p(p)`:
```
p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); y=np.log(p); return y - y.mean()
```

`_score_internal(p, ctx)`:
```
n,M,L,two_n=ctx
p=np.asarray(p,float)
P=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
b=np.fft.irfft(P*P,n=L)[:M]
if b.size < M: b=np.pad...
b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
mx = float(b.max())
return two_n*mx if finite else inf
```
Maybe due to circular convolution alias if L<2n-1? L≥3n-2, okay.

`_anti_from_P(P,p,w,L,n)`: We'll implement inside objective.

Objective function:
```
def _make_objective(ctx, kind='max', tau=None):
    n,M,L,two_n=ctx
    def obj(x):
       x=np.asarray(x,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
       p=_softmax(x)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
       b=np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)[:M]
       # clip tiny negative
       # to avoid modifying? use np.clip
       if b.min() < 0: b = np.clip(b,0,None)
       if kind == 'var':
           c = float(b.mean())
           d = b - c
           var = float(np.dot(d,d))
           val = two_n * var
           if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
           db = (two_n * 2.0) * d
           w_rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float); w_rev[:M] = db[::-1]
           W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
           T = np.fft.irfft(P * W, n=L)
           q = T[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
           gp = 2.0 * q
           dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
           grad = p * (gp - dot)
           grad -= np.mean(grad)
           if not all finite: grad=zeros
           return val, grad
       else:
           maxb=float(b.max())
           if maxb <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
           # tau ensure positive
           t = float(tau) if tau and tau>0 else maxb*1e-3
           diff = (b - maxb) / t
           # To avoid under/overflow, max diff 0
           w = np.exp(np.clip(diff, -50.0, 0.0))  # exp(-50)=1.9e-22 enough; for far lags ignore
           S = float(w.sum())
           if not finite or S <=0:
              w = np.ones(M)/M; S=1.0
           f = maxb + t * math.log(S / M)
           val = two_n * f
           w = w / S
           w_rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float); w_rev[:M]=w[::-1]
           W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
           T=np.fft.irfft(P*W, n=L)
           q=T[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
           gp = two_n * 2.0 * q
           dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
           grad = p * (gp - dot)
           grad -= np.mean(grad)
           if not all finite: grad=zeros
           return val, grad
    return obj
```
Clipping diff at -50 can bias if many lags: if all far, weights zero? At least max diff 0 => weight 1. Good. If tau very small and several lags m diff -1e-8/tau = -0.1, exp fine. If diff -50, underflow; okay. But clipping at -50 changes smooth objective slightly: log S less negative if many tails. Very small effect. Could use no clip; underflow safe. But `np.exp(-100000)` no error (underflow to0 with warning). Use `np.exp(diff)` inside `np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore')`; no clip. If diff positive due to maxb? maxb max so <=0, but floating may positive 1e-16, exp small >1, okay. Use `w = np.exp(np.minimum(diff, 0.0))` to ensure max terms? If diff tiny positive, min 0 => weight1 for those maybe slightly more active. Fine.

`S/M` maybe if only max: 1/M, log negative. If many, okay. If w sum=0 impossible.

`val` may be lower than exact due smooth bias. Fine.

For var objective, `b` clipped; d can have sum small. If p sum1, theoretical sum b=1? FFT numerical maybe not exactly. The mean c. Good.

Grad dot: For max, dot(p,gp) = two_n*2∑w p_k? Actually dot= p·(2n*2q)=2n*4? Let's not worry.

Test gradient scale for simple? Hard.

Potential scale issue: Objective for max scaled by 2n. Gradient scaled similar. L-BFGS default `ftol=1e-12`, `gtol` based gradient. Good.

However, if objective value ~1.5, `ftol=1e-12` may never stop; maxiter controls. Use `ftol=1e-10`. `gtol=1e-7`.

`_run_minimize`:
```
def _run_minimize(y0, ctx, kind, tau, iters, end_time):
    if minimize is None or iters <=0 or time.monotonic() > end_time - 1:
        return y0
    obj=_make_objective(ctx, kind, tau)
    # estimate maxiter
    y = np.asarray(y0,float).copy()
    try:
       res = minimize(obj, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                      options={'maxiter': int(iters), 'gtol':1e-7, 'ftol':1e-10, 'maxls':50, 'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       return y0
    if res is not None:
       x=res.x
       x=np.asarray(x,float)
       if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          return x - np.mean(x)
    return y
```
No time inside. We set iters conservative. Could check before. For vari objective kind 'var', tau unused.

Maybe L-BFGS line search can fail with `Line search failed` and return original? It still returns x. Good.

Now `_optimize_p` inside propose. Need access to ctx for n. Since n changes, create ctx each call. To avoid recreating per start? Create per n.

```
def _optimize_p(p0, n, rng, end_time, lp_iters=0, base_iter=250, do_var=True):
   ctx=_make_ctx(n)
   p_best=_normalize(p0)
   val_best=_score_internal(p_best,ctx)
   consider(p_best)
   y_best=_log_p(p_best)
   p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; y_work=y_best
   if lp_iters >0 and n<=600:
      ts=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0)
      for _ in range(lp_iters):
         if time.monotonic() > end_time - 60: break
         g = _lp_response(p_best, ctx)
         if g is None: break
         p mix...
         if improve: consider; no_improve=0 else break
   # L2 pre-polish if time
   if do_var and minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 120:
       y_work=_run_minimize(y_best, ctx, 'var', None, max(30, base_iter//2), end_time-60)
       p_work=_softmax(y_work); val_work=_score_internal(p_work,ctx)
       if val_work < val_best*(1-1e-10): update best
       # choose work
       if val_work > val_best*1.02:
           p_work=p_best; y_work=y_best
   elif not do_var:
       p_work=p_best; y_work=y_best
   # max stages
   rels = ...
   noimp=0
   for idx, rel in enumerate(rels):
       if time.monotonic() > end_time - 30: break
       # reset if work too bad
       if val_work > val_best*1.03 + 1e-12:
           p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; y_work=y_best
       c = val_work/(2.0*n)
       if c <= 0: break
       tau = c*rel
       # iters: early smaller, later larger
       iters = int(base_iter * (0.6 if rel>0.2 else 1.0))
       if time.monotonic() > end_time - 120:
           iters = max(30, iters//2)
       if iters <= 0: break
       y_work = _run_minimize(y_work, ctx, 'max', tau, iters, end_time-30)
       p_work = _softmax(y_work)
       val_work = _score_internal(p_work, ctx)
       if val_work < val_best*(1-1e-10):
           p_best=p_work; val_best=val_work; y_best=_log_p(p_best); noimp=0; consider(p_best)
       else:
           noimp += 1
           if noimp >= 4 and rel <= 0.03:
               # reset and maybe continue a couple fine from best
               y_work=y_best; p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; noimp=0
   # final small gradient polish? maybe skip for simplicity
   return p_best, val_best
```
We need ensure `consider` called with p_best not exceeding deadline too often. It's okay.

Now, `_resample(parent, target)`:
```
def _resample(seq,target):
 a=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
 if a.size==0: return np.ones(target)/target
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a)&(a>=0),a,0)
 if a.sum()<=0: return np.ones(target)/target
 if a.size==target: return a/a.sum()
 x=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); y=np.linspace(0,1,target)
 out=np.interp(y,x,a)
 out=np.where(out>0,out,0)
 s=out.sum()
 if s<=0: return np.ones(target)/target
 return out/s
```
If target smaller, linear interpolation might undersample; okay. For target=1024 from512, y uniform includes half points, out smooth.

`_smooth_noise`:
```
def _smooth_noise(n,rng):
    z=rng.normal(size=n)
    # box blur 3 times
    for radius in (3,5,7):
       k=np.ones(radius*2+1); k/=k.size
       z=np.convolve(z,k,'same')
    z -= z.mean()
    nz = np.linalg.norm(z)
    if nz>0: z/=nz
    # scale to unit norm in log? Then caller multiply sigma.
    return z
```
If we divide by norm, noise entries ~1/√n, sigma=0.5 gives log perturbation O(0.5/√n? wait norm fixed to1, entries std ~1/√n; multiplying sigma 0.5 gives ~0.02 for n512, very small. Maybe we want std sigma absolute. Let's not normalize. Box blur of standard normal has std reduced? Maybe not. Use absolute sigma 0.2. Let's design:
```
z = rng.normal(size=n)
for box in 5, 9: convolve with window/size
z -= z.mean()
z *= sigma
```
If box smoothing, std reduced but okay.

`_random_start`:
```
def _random_start(n,rng):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  logw=np.zeros(n)
  logw += 0.3*_smooth_noise(n,rng)
  logw += rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4) + rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)*(x-0.5) + rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)*(x-0.5)**2
  for m in (1,2,3):
      logw += rng.uniform(0,0.8)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
  return _softmax(logw)
```
This can produce near one-sided if linear strong; optimizer can fix. But random start maybe not strong.

`_jitter_start`:

```
def _jitter_start(base,rng,sigma):
  bp = _normalize(base)
  y = _log_p(bp)
  y += sigma * _smooth_noise(len(bp), rng)
  return _softmax(y)
```

```
def _make_starts(n, rng, parent_arr, max_starts):
   starts=[]
   if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size>0:
      starts.append(_resample(parent_arr,n))
   base = starts[0] if starts else _normalize(np.ones(n))
   # 1 jitter small, 1 jitter bigger maybe for n<=1024
   sigmas = (0.25, 0.6) if n<=1024 else (0.2,0.4)
   for sgn in sigmas:
      if len(starts) >= max_starts: break
      starts.append(_jitter_start(base,rng,sgn))
   if max_starts > len(starts):
      starts.append(_random_start(n,rng))
   return starts[:max_starts]
```

Let's write `_optimize_p` code:
```
def _optimize_p(p0, n, rng, end_time, lp_iters=0, base_iter=250):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    ctx = _make_ctx(n)
    p_best = p.copy(); val_best = _internal_score(p_best, ctx)
    consider(p_best)
    y_best = _log_p(p_best)
    p_work = p_best; val_work = val_best; y_work = y_best.copy()

    # LP
    if lp_iters > 0 and linprog is not None and n <= 650:
        ts=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0)
        for _ in range(int(lp_iters)):
            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 80: break
            g = _lp_response(p_best, ctx)
            if g is None: break
            bestp, bestv = p_best, val_best
            for t in ts:
                q = (1.0-t)*p_best + t*g
                q = _normalize(q)
                v = _internal_score(q, ctx)
                if v < bestv - 1e-12:
                    bestv=v; bestp=q
            if bestv < val_best - 1e-10:
                p_best=bestp; val_best=bestv; y_best=_log_p(p_best); consider(p_best)
            else:
                break
        # update work
        p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; y_work=y_best

    # var stage if time
    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 120:
        iters = max(30, base_iter//3)
        y_work = _run_minimize(y_best, ctx, 'var', None, iters, end_time - 60)
        p_work = _softmax(y_work)
        val_work = _internal_score(p_work, ctx)
        if val_work < val_best - 1e-10:
            p_best=p_work; val_best=val_work; y_best=_log_p(p_best); consider(p_best)
        if val_work > val_best * 1.03 + 1e-12:
            p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; y_work=y_best
    # max stages
    rels = (0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.025, 0.012, 0.006, 0.003, 0.0015, 0.0008)
    no_imp = 0
    for rel in rels:
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 25: break
        if val_work > val_best * 1.03 + 1e-12:
            p_work=p_best; val_work=val_best; y_work=y_best
        c = val_work / (2.0 * n)
        if not np.isfinite(c) or c <= 0: break
        tau = c * rel
        iters = int(base_iter * (0.6 if rel > 0.1 else 1.0))
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 120:
            iters = max(30, iters // 2)
        if iters <= 30: break
        y_work = _run_minimize(y_work, ctx, 'max', tau, iters, end_time - 20)
        p_work = _softmax(y_work)
        val_work = _internal_score(p_work, ctx)
        if val_work < val_best - 1e-10:
            p_best = p_work.copy(); val_best = val_work; y_best = _log_p(p_best); no_imp = 0; consider(p_best)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 5 and rel <= 0.02:
                # worse after several; stop if no improvement at fine
                break
    return p_best, val_best
```
Maybe if work after var is worse but future max from work could still improve? We reset if >3%. If slightly worse but potential, keep. Good.

But after a max stage that doesn't improve, we continue to smaller tau from current work. If current work exact slightly worse, smaller tau may still improve; okay. If it wanders, reset after >3%.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None


def _normalize(raw):
    a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a /= s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _make_ctx(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        L = int(np.fft.next_fast_len(3 * n - 2))
    except Exception:
        L = 1 << max(1, (3 * n - 2) - 1).bit_length()
    if L < m:
        L = int(np.fft.next_fast_len(m))
    return n, m, L, 2.0 * float(n)


def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = e / s
    p = np.where(p > 1e-300, p, 1e-300)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def _log_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(p > 1e-300, p, 1e-300)
    y = np.log(p)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    return y


def _internal_score(p, ctx):
    n, m, L, two_n = ctx
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(P * P, n=L)[:m]
    if b.size < m:
        b = np.concatenate([b, np.zeros(m - b.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    mx = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(two_n * mx)


def _make_objective(ctx, kind="max", tau=None, var_weight=0.0):
    n, m, L, two_n = ctx

    def obj(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        p = _softmax(x)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
        b = np.fft.irfft(P * P, n=L)[:m]
        if b.size < m:
            b = np.concatenate([b, np.zeros(m - b.size, dtype=np.float64)])
        b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)

        val = 0.0
        gp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        if kind in ("max", "combo"):
            maxb = float(b.max())
            if maxb <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxb):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = float(tau) if tau is not None and tau > 0.0 else maxb * 1e-3
            diff = (b - maxb) / t
            diff = np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
            w = np.exp(diff)
            S = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
                w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
                S = 1.0
            fmax = maxb + t * math.log(S / float(m))
            val += two_n * fmax
            w = w / S

            wr = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            wr[:m] = w[::-1]
            W = np.fft.rfft(wr, n=L)
            T = np.fft.irfft(P * W, n=L)
            q = T[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            gp += two_n * 2.0 * q

        if kind in ("var", "combo"):
            c = float(b.mean())
            d = b - c
            var = float(np.dot(d, d))
            val += two_n * var_weight * var
            db = two_n * var_weight * 2.0 * d
            wr = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            wr[:m] = db[::-1]
            W = np.fft.rfft(wr, n=L)
            T = np.fft.irfft(P * W, n=L)
            q = T[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            gp += 2.0 * q

        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        g = p * (gp - dot)
        g -= float(np.mean(g))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return float(val), g

    return obj


def _run_minimize(y0, ctx, kind, tau, iters, end_time, var_weight=0.0):
    if minimize is None or iters <= 0:
        return y0
    if time.monotonic() > end_time - 2.0:
        return y0
    y0 = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64)
    obj = _make_objective(ctx, kind=kind, tau=tau, var_weight=var_weight)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj,
            y0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(max(5, iters)),
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return y0
    if res is not None:
        x = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", y0), dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.size == y0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
            return x
    return y0


def _lp_response(p, ctx):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    n, m, L, two_n = ctx
    if n > 650:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(p > 1e-12, p, 1e-12)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = p / s

    c = _internal_score(p, ctx)
    if not np.isfinite(c) or c <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = np.empty((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    scale = 1.0 / c
    for k in range(m):
        if k < n:
            A[k, :k + 1] = (p[:k + 1][::-1] * scale)
        else:
            start = k - n + 1
            A[k, start:n] = (p[start:n][::-1] * scale)

    cc = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - 1e-5 * (p * two_n)
    b_ub = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = (0.0, 1.0e6)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(
            cc,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(cc, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="simplex")
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    g = g / s
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize(g)


def _resample(seq, target):
    target = int(max(1, target))
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == target:
        return a / s
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target)
    out = np.interp(y, x, a)
    out = np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out)


def _smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma=0.4):
    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    for radius in (3, 5):
        k = np.ones(2 * radius + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        k /= float(k.size)
        z = np.convolve(z, k, mode="same")
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    sd = float(np.std(z))
    if sd <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sd):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return z * (sigma / sd)


def _random_start(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    lw = _smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma=0.35)
    lw += rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
    lw += rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2) * (x - 0.5)
    lw += rng.uniform(-1.4, 1.4) * (x - 0.5) ** 2
    for mm in (1, 2, 3):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.7)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        lw += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * mm * x + phase)
    return _softmax(lw)


def _jitter_start(base, rng, sigma=0.35):
    p = _normalize(base)
    y = _log_p(p)
    y = y + _smooth_noise(y.size, rng, sigma=sigma)
    return _softmax(y)


def _optimize_p(p0, n, rng, end_time, lp_iters=0, base_iter=200):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    ctx = _make_ctx(n)
    p_best = p.copy()
    val_best = _internal_score(p_best, ctx)
    consider(p_best)

    y_best = _log_p(p_best)
    p_work = p_best.copy()
    y_work = y_best.copy()
    val_work = val_best

    if lp_iters > 0 and n <= 650:
        ts = (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
        for _ in range(int(max(0, lp_iters))):
            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 70.0:
                break
            g = _lp_response(p_best, ctx)
            if g is None:
                break
            bestp, bestv = p_best, val_best
            for tt in ts:
                q = (1.0 - tt) * p_best + tt * g
                q = _normalize(q)
                v = _internal_score(q, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(v) and v < bestv - 1e-12:
                    bestv, bestp = v, q
            if bestv < val_best - 1e-10:
                p_best, val_best = bestp, bestv
                y_best = _log_p(p_best)
                consider(p_best)
            else:
                break
        p_work, y_work, val_work = p_best.copy(), y_best.copy(), val_best

    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 110.0:
        iters = max(25, base_iter // 3)
        y_work = _run_minimize(y_best, ctx, kind="var", tau=None, iters=iters, end_time=end_time - 50.0)
        p_work = _softmax(y_work)
        val_work = _internal_score(p_work, ctx)
        if val_work < val_best - 1e-10:
            p_best, val_best = p_work.copy(), val_work
            y_best = _log_p(p_best)
            consider(p_best)
        if val_work > val_best * 1.03 + 1e-12:
            p_work, y_work, val_work = p_best.copy(), y_best.copy(), val_best

    rels = (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.025, 0.012, 0.006, 0.003, 0.0015, 0.0008)
    no_imp = 0
    for rel in rels:
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 25.0:
            break
        if val_work > val_best * 1.035 + 1e-12:
            p_work, y_work, val_work = p_best.copy(), y_best.copy(), val_best
        c = val_work / (2.0 * float(n))
        if not np.isfinite(c) or c <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = c * rel
        iters = int(base_iter * (0.6 if rel > 0.10 else 1.0))
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 120.0:
            iters = max(25, iters // 2)
        if iters < 25:
            break
        y_work = _run_minimize(y_work, ctx, kind="max", tau=tau, iters=iters, end_time=end_time - 20.0)
        p_work = _softmax(y_work)
        val_work = _internal_score(p_work, ctx)
        if val_work < val_best - 1e-10:
            p_best, val_best = p_work.copy(), val_work
            y_best = _log_p(p_best)
            no_imp = 0
            consider(p_best)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 5 and rel <= 0.02:
                break

    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 70.0:
        y_work = _run_minimize(y_best, ctx, kind="var", tau=None, iters=max(25, base_iter // 3), end_time=end_time - 30.0)
        p_work = _softmax(y_work)
        val_work = _internal_score(p_work, ctx)
        if val_work < val_best - 1e-10:
            p_best, val_best = p_work.copy(), val_work
            y_best = _log_p(p_best)
            consider(p_best)

    return p_best, val_best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_end = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    work_end = hard_end - 30.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_list = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def consider(seq):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score
        try:
            a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if a.size == 0:
                return
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
            a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
                return
            a = a / s
            lst = [float(x) for x in a]
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
                best_score = val
                best_list = lst
        except Exception:
            pass

    q4 = np.array([0.2, 0.4, 0.2, 0.2], dtype=np.float64)
    q4 = _normalize(q4)
    consider(q4)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hs is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(arr)):
                parent_arr = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        consider(_normalize(parent_arr))
    else:
        parent_arr = q4

    base_len = int(parent_arr.size) if parent_arr is not None else 512
    if not (128 <= base_len <= 4096):
        base_len = 512

    tasks = []
    seen = set()
    for mult in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        n = int(base_len * mult)
        if n in seen or n < 64 or n > 4096:
            continue
        seen.add(n)
        if n <= 700:
            alloc = 450.0
            starts_max = 3
            lp = 3
            base_iter = 220
        elif n <= 1500:
            alloc = 300.0
            starts_max = 2
            lp = 0
            base_iter = 130
        elif n <= 3000:
            alloc = 180.0
            starts_max = 1
            lp = 0
            base_iter = 80
        else:
            alloc = 90.0
            starts_max = 1
            lp = 0
            base_iter = 50
        tasks.append((n, alloc, starts_max, lp, base_iter))

    for idx, (n, alloc, starts_max, lp_iters, base_iter) in enumerate(tasks):
        if time.monotonic() > work_end:
            break
        phase_end = min(work_end, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        if phase_end - time.monotonic() < 30.0:
            break

        starts = []
        try:
            if parent_arr is not None:
                starts.append(_resample(parent_arr, n))
            base_start = starts[0] if starts else _normalize(np.ones(n))

            if n <= 1500:
                starts.append(_jitter_start(base_start, rng, sigma=0.25))
                starts.append(_jitter_start(base_start, rng, sigma=0.55))
            else:
                starts.append(_jitter_start(base_start, rng, sigma=0.20))

            if len(starts) < starts_max:
                starts.append(_random_start(n, rng))
            while len(starts) < starts_max:
                starts.append(_jitter_start(base_start, rng, sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.7))))
        except Exception:
            starts = [_normalize(np.ones(n))]

        starts = starts[: max(1, int(starts_max))]
        per_start = max(30.0, (phase_end - time.monotonic()) / float(len(starts)))

        for i, st in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > phase_end:
                break
            if i == len(starts) - 1:
                sub_end = phase_end
            else:
                sub_end = min(phase_end, time.monotonic() + per_start + 10.0)
            if sub_end - time.monotonic() < 25.0:
                break
            try:
                _optimize_p(st, n, rng, end_time=sub_end, lp_iters=lp_iters if i == 0 else 0, base_iter=base_iter)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_list is None:
        best_list = [float(x) for x in q4]

    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 10.0:
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                best_list = [float(x) for x in q4]
        except Exception:
            best_list = [float(x) for x in q4]

    try:
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_end - 2.0 and best_list is not None and len(best_list) > 8:
            fallback = [float(x) for x in q4]
            fv = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
            if np.isfinite(fv):
                best_list = fallback
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_list] if best_list is not None else [1.0]
```
